Every part of the garment, from the collar band to the leg openings, is printed in the outfit's own cloth. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.

An attached hood at the neck is printed as a helmet. Front and back are both fully printed, the back showing how the outfit looks from behind. The print is cut to the garment's exact shape, so the neck, shoulders, sleeves and leg openings are all covered in the outfit's own fabric. The print is cut exactly to the garment, neck, shoulders, armholes, sleeves and hem are all covered in the outfit's own fabric. It is a one-piece romper tee, a T-shirt top attached to matching shorts, with the outfit's top and bottom printed as one continuous piece. All textures, transparencies and sheens are printed patterns on solid cotton. The picture is only the garment, its cloth, seams, buttons and trims laid flat across the whole garment like a sewing pattern. The suit is the whole lunar miner outfit, not just a top, with a white pressure suit with dust stains, a helmet (as the hood), boots (as the feet), a drill sewn or attached to the suit. It is shown in full, from the neckline down to the ankle cuffs. Novelty embellishments, scalloped felt edging and a rainbow of rickrack, ribbon and braid trim. It reads as a joyful, exaggerated novelty onesie, stuffed with texture and detail.
